# Near misses and US drafts

C that is not linked and not counted: a starting point. One file per function,
`nonmatching/<program>/func_XXXXXXXX.c` (`<program>` is `frontbin`, `boot_elf`, `i5bootn` or `levels`,
the name is the PAL address), with a comment at its top saying where it came from and what still
differs. Move it into `src/` once the full build prints `MATCH` with it in place, and delete its
row here. `tests/check_repository.py` checks that every file has a row.

A draft that uses inline asm (beyond `.extern` size hints), `volatile` (beyond named hardware memory and the literal EE hardware register and scratchpad addresses AGENTS.md allows), a `#define` alias or a hardcoded address is a study aid, not a route to a match: those are not allowed in matched C, so such a function stays here until plain C matches.

Files that start with `/* US draft` are rac3-uya-decomp's C for that function (commit 13cf5c5),
with names moved to PAL addresses where the address maps are sure of them
(`tools/port_from_us.py`, docs/history/porting-from-us.md). They were not carried for the reason in their row:

- **flags**: the US match used flags of its own for this one function (a "single-function
  override" or `@ps2as` range), which this repository does not allow (docs/build/build-fidelity.md);
- **differs**: the PAL function's bytes are not the US ones, or the C did not build to them here;
- **symbols**: a name in the C has no PAL address yet.

`functional.tsv` lists the US drafts OpenRAC's native port of NTSC-U reads in place of the
assembly (`tools/us_names.py --candidates`, docs/build/versions.md): C that matched NTSC-U, for a
function NTSC-U has and src/us/ has no C for.

FE-A1 (2026-11-22): func_003EE760's plain C (its twin, the same bytes) is 18 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (src/boot_elf/text/3EE518.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses`, no `@ps2as`): the default assembler builds the 0.01f constant differently, and with `@ps2as` the twin's 10 differences would remain; NM-FE2 (2026-11-22): func_003EE760's best plain C (its row) with the names moved is 15 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (src/boot_elf/text/3EE518.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses`, no `@ps2as`), and 6 with `--as ps2as` (the same 6 as its twin). Without `@ps2as` the 0.01f is a `$gp` load instead of retail's `lui`/`ori`/`mtc1`; the line is not changed while the twin does not match; RT-C (2026-11-22, upstream 70e001ab): the draft file now holds the twin's plain C (15 differences with its file's line, 6 with `--as ps2as`, tools/check.py `--target boot_elf`; the same 15 with `--no-context`); the US form with pins and asm barriers is kept below it in a comment (MATCH with `--as ps2as` only through those forbidden forms). The 6 are the twin's scheduling blocker (see `func_003EE760`); the natural store order is 18 (10 with `--as ps2as`). The flag line is not changed while neither function matches; NM-R21 (2026-11-23, upstream 1dbb16a7): re-measured 15 differences (tools/check.py `--target boot_elf`, its file's line) and 6 with `--as ps2as`; not ported further because the twin `func_003EE760` is still 6 (its row lists the forms tried).; CLOSEOUT attempt 1 of 2 (2026-11-24, upstream 626eebd0): NEAR MISS, no change. Re-measured 15 differences with its file's line and 6 with `--as ps2as` (tools/check.py `--target boot_elf`; the same with `--no-context`). Six more plain-C wordings compile to the same 6 wrong words with `--as ps2as` (15 without): the stores in a `static inline` helper, an inline helper that returns the object, a `register` local (no asm) for the stores' base, the parameter as `int` cast at each use, the stores and the result as one comma expression in the `return`, and half the stores through a result local. In every one the return copy is still the last insn in sched1's order (`tools/sched_explain.py --as ps2as`), so a store whose value dies there wins the cycle-5 tie; without `@ps2as` the 0.01f `$gp` load remains. Not ported further: the lever would have to move the copy ahead of the stores in the RTL stream, and no plain-C form found does that. |

| `func_003EE760` | frontbin | US draft of `func_003E91F8` | its file (3ED988.c) is `@ps2as` now; the draft matches only with register pins and an asm barrier; plain C is 10 diff: 0.5f/0.01f land in `$f1`/`$f0` and the vtable in `$v0` where retail uses `$f0`/`$f1` and `$a0`. FE-A1 (2026-11-22): plain C is still 10 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (now src/frontbin/3EE160.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses @ps2as`), the same with a struct layout, float locals, every store order, the result in a local, and `func_003ED988` called with or without the argument: the register choice is fixed before scheduling; NM-FE2 (2026-11-22): 6 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (src/frontbin/3EE160.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses @ps2as`), the same with `--no-context`. The draft's body as plain stores, no pins or barriers, `func_003ED988();` first, then in the order 0x18 = 0.5f, 0x2C = 0, 0x34 = 0.01f, 0x1C = 0.5f, 8 = `D_001D9738`, 0x30 = 0x80F00000, 0x38 = 0.01f, then `return object;`. Every register now matches retail; only the second scheduling pass differs: retail puts `move $v0, $s0` before the vtable store (ours puts the 0x1C store first) and `sw $zero, 0x2C` last (ours before the 0x34 store). All 5040 orders of the seven plain stores: 29 reach 6, none lower; the stores in pairs (split or chained, either order, 1920 forms), a struct layout, `func_003ED988(object)` and a float zero at 0x2C also stay at 6; RT-C (2026-11-22, upstream 70e001ab): the draft file now holds that plain C (6 differences, tools/check.py, its file's line, the same with `--no-context`); the US form with pins and asm barriers is kept below it in a comment, as information (it is MATCH only through those forbidden forms). The blocker is the first scheduling pass's register-pressure ranking (`-dS` dumps): among equally urgent ready insns it prefers a store whose source register dies there (weight -1) over the return copy `move $v0, $s0` (weight 0: it sets `$v0`), and some such store is always ready in the cycle the copy becomes eligible (the vtable `la`, `li 0x80F00000` and `0.5f` are all loaded in the two cycles before). The second pass then keeps that order (ties by original order), so `move $v0` follows the first store. Retail needs the copy before every store, which also gives the vtable `la` `$a0` instead of `$v0`. The natural order (8, 0x30, 0x18, 0x1C, 0x34, 0x38, 0x2C) gives retail's exact store order (8, 0x30, 0x1C, 0x38, 0x18, 0x34, 0x2C: the dying use of each value first) but the copy lands after the vtable store, so `$v0` takes the vtable and `$f0`/`$f1` swap: 10 differences. Also 10: a struct layout in the natural order, `func_003ED988(object)` with chained pairs, a copy of the result before or after the call (`ret = object`, or stores through a copy); named float locals: 26; NM-R21 (2026-11-23, upstream 1dbb16a7): still 6 differences (tools/check.py `--target frontbin`, its file's line, the same with `--no-context`). The same 6: the callee declared `void *func_003ED988(void *)` and called with `object`; its result taken as a local; a scoped base local holding the object (created before the call) for the stores; the result in a local assigned after the call. Worse: a reassigned pointer local (8), the body in `for (;;) { ... return object; }` (12), the return written above the stores and reached by `goto` (22), the two floats in locals set before the call (25). `tools/sched_explain.py` shows the return copy is the last insn in sched1's source order (luid 13 of 13), so it loses every tie with a store ready in its cycle. |

The code is an 8-byte remnant (`addiu $2,$0,1; nop`, as boot_elf's `func_003F2770`), and config/inventory.tsv reads `frontbin .text 003F23B8 4 remnant`, but the marker's .s unit is 436 bytes: it also holds 428 bytes of frontbin's ELF .shstrtab and section-header table (from file offset 0x21DD40), because splat's frontbin text segment (config/splat/frontbin.yaml) runs to the end of the file (0x21DEEC). A `LINKER_REMNANT` marking builds (3 MATCH, pr_check frontbin OK) but would label ELF metadata as code. Recipe: end .text at 0x3F23C0 (file offset 0x21DD40) in config/splat/frontbin.yaml, which makes it a plain 8-byte remnant; a segment change is the maintainers' call |
